Have them import scripts/datasets/_common.load_jsonl or a sibling helper that adds an `annotate_line` flag. Migrate the remaining copies only when their script is touched, and delete dead scripts first.
verify-note: The count reproduces: 47 load/read/iter_jsonl definitions in non-autopilot scripts plus 2 in src. The semantic divergence is real (datasets/_common.py:27 returns [] when the file is missing and rejects non-dicts; summarize_real_task_corpus.py:24 raises and accepts any value; bep_ab.py:45 is a one-liner). The metrics confirm the 3 identical graph_router copies. But the proposed API is not behavior-preserving as written: datasets/_common.load_jsonl also mutates each row with `row["_source_line"] = lineno`, which the proposed flags do not cover. Most of the 47 sit in one-off analysis/experiment scripts, so a cross-tree helper migration costs review time for little benefit compared with deleting dead scripts (A5-23).
verification: Golden tests for read_jsonl covering every flag combination. For each migrated script, run its existing unit test if one exists. `grep -rnE 'def _?(load|read|iter)_jsonl' scripts | wc -l` drops from 47 to 1.

## A5-14 [sev 1] [best_practice/refactor] [effort S] [behav none] [partially] loc_delta=-60
**Atomic-write helpers are reimplemented with different durability guarantees (fixed vs pid-unique tmp names, fsync vs none)**
files: O:scripts/server/env_override.py:187, O:scripts/server/fleet_markers.py:70, O:scripts/server/runtime_facts_manifest.py:309, O:scripts/server/stack_log_banner.py:155, O:scripts/lab/record_verdict.py:61, O:scripts/benchmark/eval_batch_serving_evaltower_window.py:319, O:src/runtime/quiescence.py:164
evidence: There are 6 definitions in non-autopilot scripts, 10 in autopilot (8 byte-identical `_atomic_write` in operator_candidates per the metrics) and 3 in src, plus inline copies (stack_log_banner.py:155-158). They are not equivalent. env_override.write_json_atomic (:187) uses a FIXED tmp name `path.name + '.tmp'` with no fsync. fleet_markers._atomic_write (:70) uses a pid-suffixed tmp plus fsync, and its docstring explains why: uvicorn workers 'can read 0-byte or half-written files and crash'. eval_batch_serving_evaltower_window (:319-345) also fsyncs the parent directory. lab/record_verdict (:61) uses pid tmp without fsync. A fixed tmp name means two concurrent writers truncate each other's tmp before os.replace. Which guarantee you get depends on which file you copied from.
proposal: Add one `atomic_write_bytes/text/json(path, data, *, fsync=True, mode=None)` in a shared module (src/util/atomic.py, which A5-03 makes importable from both trees) using a pid+uuid tmp name in the same dir, fsync of file and parent, and os.replace. Replace the copies. Operator-candidate scripts that must stay self-contained (hash-pinned ratification inputs) can be left alone, with a comment saying so.
CORRECTED: Add one helper with an explicit `fsync` parameter and a pid+uuid tmp name. Migrate fleet_markers, runtime_facts_manifest and env_override, keeping each site's current fsync behaviour explicit. Leave the hash-pinned operator_candidates copies alone.
verify-note: The divergence is real. env_override.write_json_atomic (:187-190) uses a fixed `.tmp` name and no fsync. fleet_markers._atomic_write (:70-84) uses a pid tmp plus fsync, with the uvicorn-worker rationale. record_verdict (:61-65) uses a pid tmp without fsync. The count is 8 defs in 7 non-autopilot script files, not 6. The claimed concurrency hazard is not demonstrated: write_json_atomic's only callers are env_override.py:269 and embedder_env_override.py:115, which are operator-invoked one-shot override records. A default of fsync=True would also add latency if adopted by any request-path writer (e.g. src/api/routes/dashboard.py:3348).
verification: Unit tests for the helper: concurrent writers via multiprocessing produce a valid final file; the tmp is cleaned up on exception. After migration, run the existing tests for fleet_markers, runtime_facts_manifest and env_override.

Since then, maintenance/reseed_episodic_store.py says the store is rebuilt 'on the fixed contract' (new schema, re-embedded vectors), so seeders written against the old schema are probably stale.
proposal: Fix the usage paths (python -m scripts.memory.<m>). Then have the memory owner run each seeder with --dry-run against the current schema. Delete the ones that fail or have been superseded by reseed_episodic_store, and mark docs/chapters/09-memory-seeding.md as historical.
verify-note: The usage paths are wrong: migrate_to_faiss.py:9 says `python scripts/migrate_to_faiss.py`, q_scorer_runner.py:12-21 says `scripts/q_scorer_runner.py`, seed_success_patterns.py:8 is similar, and docs/chapters/09-memory-seeding.md:181 says `python scripts/seed_memory_from_logs.py`. All of these files live under scripts/memory/. q_scorer_runner.py:24 even documents a crontab line pointing at the non-existent scripts/q_scorer_runner.py, so any host cron copied from it is silently broken. Grep finds q_scorer_runner referenced only by itself, a refactor doc and R research prose.
verification: The usage-path scan returns 0 missing. `uv run python -m scripts.memory.seed_success_patterns --dry-run` runs (on host) or is removed.

# A6 — orchestrator test suite  (findings kept: 20, refuted: 1, bloat_est: 5200)

The suite is large (16,777 test functions, 46k asserts) and carefully reasoned, but it was written to run on one host and nowhere else. Hermeticity is opt-out: eight-plus autouse fixtures across two conftests each patch one subsystem after a production incident, while 256 monkeypatches of module-level *_PATH constants and 357 sys.path inserts hold the rest together. Several tests can still harm the shared host. The worst is the AP-54 fence suite, which runs truncate, unlink and rename payloads against the REAL /mnt/raid0/llm/epyc-root/wiki/INDEX.md and depends on the code under test to block them. Other tests write fixed-name directories into the production tmp folder, read the live autopilot_state.json, and leave MagicMock/ and ni07_*.json files in the checkout. The ni07 file is opened with mode "x", so that test fails on every run after the first. There is no CI and the run commands disagree with each other. `make test` skips 57 test files (about 20.4k LOC), the Justfile test recipes are stubs, and nine modules (108+ tests) skip themselves when CI=true, so adding GitHub Actions as-is would silently drop them. Meanwhile the conftest memory guard calls pytest.exit on any machine with less than 100 GB free unless CI is set. A lot of test LOC buys little coverage: 89 tests contain no assertion, 183 have only weak ones, and 100% line-coverage floors on 9 files have produced mock-heavy "_branching/_additional" files. About 1.9k LOC tests modules that nothing in production imports, plus 430 LOC of "tripwire" tests that fail if that dead code is deleted. About 50 source-text tests (9 of them slice the 2,901-line `_run_loop_inner`) will break on the formatting and decomposition refactors this program plans. Bloat estimate: about 5,200 test LOC can be deleted or merged with no loss of coverage. A further 11.6k LOC (E8 one-off campaign tests) can be quarantined from the default run, and about 3.0k LOC of production code is dead and kept alive only by its tests.

## A6-03 [sev 4] [tests/bug] [effort S] [behav intended] [partially] loc_delta=-25
**`CI` env var overloaded: it disables the host memory guard AND silently skips 108+ tests; without it, any non-production machine is refused**
files: O:tests/conftest.py:40, O:tests/conftest.py:43, O:tests/conftest.py:96, O:tests/integration/test_archive_pipeline.py:28, O:tests/integration/test_documents_route.py:16, O:tests/integration/test_sessions_route.py:15, O:tests/unit/test_repl_file_exploration.py:9, O:tests/unit/test_registry_loader.py:412
evidence: conftest (HEAD) line 40 sets MEMORY_THRESHOLD_GB = 100 and line 43 sets IS_CI = CI=='true' or ORCHESTRATOR_MOCK_MODE=='true'. When not IS_CI, pytest_configure calls `pytest.exit(..., returncode=1)` (line 96) if psutil reports less than 100 GB available. This container has 11 GB available (`free -g`), so a plain `pytest` exits before collection. The same CI flag is reused by 9 test files to skip themselves: test_archive_pipeline (26 tests, module-level skip, one /mnt/raid0 mention and that one is in a comment), test_documents_route (11 tests, zero /mnt/raid0 refs), test_sessions_route (15), test_repl_file_exploration (24), test_repl_file_mutation (19), test_output_spill (13), plus skipif helpers in test_registry_loader.py:412 and test_dependencies.py:268. GitHub Actions sets CI=true automatically, so adding CI would silently remove at least 108 tests from the very run meant to protect them.
proposal: Split the two meanings. Key the memory guard to the production host, e.g. run it only when `Path('/mnt/raid0/llm').is_dir()` or ORCHESTRATOR_PROD_HOST=1, and downgrade it to a warning when -n is not set. Replace every `os.environ.get('CI')` skip with a resource-based marker `@pytest.mark.host_paths(<path>)`, implemented once in conftest's pytest_collection_modifyitems as 'skip if path absent'. Then fix the tests that only need a configurable allowed root (A6-09) so they run everywhere.
CORRECTED: Proposal as written, with two changes. Convert cache_hits:283 to a timing marker (e.g. perf_benchmark, deselected in CI), not host_paths. The REPL modules need no production change: they can use tmp_path today (see A6-09), so drop their skips outright.
verify-note: Verified against HEAD conftest. MEMORY_THRESHOLD_GB=100 is at :40, IS_CI (CI=='true' or ORCHESTRATOR_MOCK_MODE=='true') at :43, and pytest.exit at :96. The guard is still present in the working-tree version (now :116/:119). This box has about 14 GB available, so a plain run exits. I counted the CI-keyed skips: module-level in archive_pipeline (26), documents_route (11), sessions_route (15), repl_file_exploration (24) and repl_file_mutation (19); class-level in registry_loader:415, dependencies:271, cache_hits:283 and output_spill:166. output_spill skips only the 3-test class TestExactRecallThroughRepl, not 13 tests. The total is roughly 98 plus three class skips, not 108+. The cache_hits:283 skip has a different reason ('Performance benchmarks unreliable on CI runners'), so a host_paths marker is the wrong replacement for it.
verification: `grep -rn 'os.environ.get("CI")' tests` should return 0. On a 16 GB box, `CI= pytest --collect-only -q tests | tail -1` should collect without exiting. `CI=true pytest --collect-only -q tests/integration/test_documents_route.py` should collect 11 tests, not 0.

## A6-04 [sev 4] [tests/test] [effort M] [behav none] [confirmed] loc_delta=40
**No CI, and the four documented ways to run the tests disagree; `make test` skips 57 test files**
files: O:.github/dependabot.yml, O:Makefile:17, O:Makefile:268, O:Makefile:276, O:Justfile:84, O:Justfile:89, O:CONTRIBUTING.md:36, O:pyproject.toml:154
evidence: .github/ contains only dependabot.yml, so there is no workflow at all. `make gates` (Makefile:17-19) runs schema/shellcheck/format/lint and adds integration-sanity only `ifneq ($(CI),)`, so locally it runs zero tests. `make test` = `unit` (pytest tests/unit, :268) + `integration` (pytest tests/integration, :276). That omits the 52 top-level tests/test_*.py files (18,873 LOC, e.g. test_reviewer_contract.py, test_candidate_security.py) and tests/classifiers, tests/retrieval and tests/vision (5 files, 1,536 LOC). The Justfile `unit` and `integration` recipes (:84-91) are stubs that echo '(no unit tests yet)'. CONTRIBUTING.md:36, CLAUDE.md:46 and README.md:186 all say `pytest tests/ -n 8`. Duplicate test basenames make the split visible: tests/test_shapekeyed_step2_smoke.py (528 LOC) and tests/unit/test_shapekeyed_step2_smoke.py (763 LOC) both test the same scripts/autopilot/shapekeyed_step2_smoke.py, but only one is in `make test`.
proposal: Make `pytest tests/` the single entry point: `make test` runs `$(PY) -m pytest tests -n 4`, and the Justfile stubs delegate to make or are deleted. Fold top-level tests/test_*.py into tests/unit or tests/integration (merge the two shapekeyed files). Add .github/workflows/tests.yml: on [push, pull_request]; ubuntu-latest; actions/setup-python 3.13 (matching .venv) plus a 3.11 matrix leg (requires-python>=3.11); `uv sync --frozen --extra dev`. Run `uv run pytest tests -n 4 --strict-markers --timeout=120 -m 'not requires_server and not requires_live_models' --junitxml=junit.xml -p no:cacheprovider`, then a `git status --porcelain --ignored` must-be-empty step (A6-02). Run `ruff check` on the whole repo, and `ruff format --check` only on changed files until fact (b) is resolved. Gate merges on the test job once A6-02, A6-03 and A6-09 are fixed and fact (a) lands.
verify-note: .github/ contains only dependabot.yml. In the Makefile, GATES_DEPS adds integration-sanity only when CI is non-empty (:15-18), unit runs pytest tests/unit (:267-273), integration runs pytest tests/integration (:275-281), and test runs unit+integration (:283). The Justfile unit and integration recipes are echo stubs (:83-91). CONTRIBUTING.md:36, CLAUDE.md:46 and README.md:186 all say `pytest tests/ -n 8`. I counted 52 top-level tests/test_*.py files (18,873 LOC) plus 5 files in classifiers/retrieval/vision, i.e. 57 files outside `make test`. tests/test_shapekeyed_step2_smoke.py (528 LOC, 26 tests) and tests/unit/test_shapekeyed_step2_smoke.py (763 LOC, 28 tests) both import scripts.autopilot.shapekeyed_step2_smoke. One nit: addopts is '-v --tb=short'.
verification: `pytest --collect-only -q tests | tail -1` and `make test` (with -q) should report the same number of tests. The workflow run should be green on a PR. `just unit` should run real tests or no longer exist.

## A6-01 [sev 3] [tests/bug] [effort S] [behav intended] [confirmed] loc_delta=15
**Kernel-fence tests aim destructive payloads at the REAL governance wiki and production tmp**
files: O:tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py:847, O:tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py:851, O:tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py:862, O:tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py:945, O:tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py:981, O:tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py:987, O:tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py:1148, O:src/repl_environment/knowledge_fence.py:234
evidence: `_WIKI` (line 851) builds '/mnt/raid0/llm/epyc-root/wiki/INDEX.md' at runtime, so the payload deliberately bypasses the source pre-check and only the kernel layer stands in the way. `test_kernel_enforcement_denies_every_leak` (961) runs payloads including `os.rename(p,'/mnt/raid0/llm/tmp/rn_'+pid)` (947), `os.link` and `shutil.copy` of python3 into /mnt/raid0/llm/tmp. `test_kernel_enforcement_denies_writes_to_fenced` (987) runs `open(p,'r+').truncate(0)`, `os.truncate(p,0)` and `os.unlink(p)` (981) against the same tracked epyc-root file (`git ls-files wiki/INDEX.md` confirms it is tracked). Both run whenever `_real_wiki_present()` is true, i.e. only on the production host, so a regression in the Landlock shim (the thing under test) moves, truncates or deletes a governance file other sessions are reading. The `_container_tmp_with_real_fenced_child` fixture (1148-1170) mkdtemps inside production /mnt/raid0/llm/tmp. `test_enforcement_level_is_landlock_here` (862) asserts ENFORCEMENT == LANDLOCK unconditionally, and its comment says 'This container (kernel 6.14, non-root)'; this container runs kernel 6.18 as root. `ENFORCEMENT = fk.probe_enforcement()` runs at import (847). The file already has a hermetic `tree` fixture (line 46) that builds a miniature llm root with a wiki, and fence roots are keyed on `_llm_root()`, which comes from get_config().paths.llm_root / ORCHESTRATOR_PATHS_LLM_ROOT (knowledge_fence.py:234-240).
proposal: Point the kernel-enforcement tests at the `tree` fixture: monkeypatch ORCHESTRATOR_PATHS_LLM_ROOT=str(tree), call reset_config() and kf.refresh_roots(), and build `_WIKI`/`_WIKI_DIR` from str(tree) (still assembled at runtime so the source pre-check is bypassed). Replace the hardcoded '/mnt/raid0/llm/tmp' targets with a tmp_path sibling, and make `_CONTAINER_TMP` a tmp_path-based TMPDIR. Change `test_enforcement_level_is_landlock_here` to skip when `fk.landlock_abi() is None`. Move ENFORCEMENT into a session fixture so collection does not spawn subprocesses.
CORRECTED: Use the existing `llm_root` fixture (line 578), not `tree` (line 46). llm_root already builds worktrees/mains/mainA/wiki, which _WIKI_DIR needs, and it already monkeypatches kf._llm_root and calls kf.refresh_roots(). The parent computes the roots and passes them to the child through the hook roots file, so the parent-side patch reaches the kernel shim. Write 'EPYC Root' into the tmp INDEX.md so the _LEAK check still means something. Build the payload paths from str(llm_root), still concatenated at runtime. Replace the /mnt/raid0/llm/tmp targets with tmp_path siblings. Skip test_enforcement_level_is_landlock_here when fk.landlock_abi() is None, and delete the stale 'kernel 6.14, non-root' comment.
verify-note: Opened test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py. ENFORCEMENT = fk.probe_enforcement() is at :847. _WIKI and _WIKI_DIR are at :853/:854, not :851. They build /mnt/raid0/llm/epyc-root/wiki/INDEX.md and /mnt/raid0/llm/worktrees/mains/mainA/wiki at runtime. Payloads os_rename_read (:947), hardlink, copied_interp, cp_parent, py_truncate, os_truncate and py_unlink (:981) all target the real files or /mnt/raid0/llm/tmp, gated only by _real_wiki_present(). test_enforcement_level_is_landlock_here (:862) asserts unconditionally. _container_tmp_with_real_fenced_child (:1152) runs mkdtemp in /mnt/raid0/llm/tmp. The danger is real, but it needs a regression in the shim under test, and the wiki file is tracked in git, so severity drops from 4 to 3. ENFORCEMENT at import costs only 55 ms (I measured it), so moving it into a fixture is cosmetic.
verification: `grep -n '/mnt/raid0' tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py` should show only classifier string inputs (lines 77-99, 285-288, 469-492, 597, 801-804, 906-910). Run `.venv/bin/python -m pytest -p no:cacheprovider tests/unit/test_ap54_eval_knowledge_fence.py -k kernel_enforcement` off-host: the tests should now RUN (not skip) and pass. Leak/write cases should report DENIED against the tmp wiki.

## A6-02 [sev 3] [tests/bug] [effort S] [behav intended] [partially] loc_delta=20
**Tests write into the checkout; one is non-idempotent and fails on every run after the first; the leak was gitignored instead of fixed**
files: O:tests/unit/test_utm_v3_conformance.py:15, O:tests/unit/test_utm_v3_conformance.py:124, O:tests/unit/test_graph_compaction_budgets.py:418, O:tests/unit/test_graph_compaction_budgets.py:438, O:tests/unit/test_graph_compaction_budgets.py:520, O:src/graph/compaction.py:112, O:.gitignore:137, O:src/orchestration/delegation_reports.py:22
evidence: test_utm_v3_conformance.py:15 sets `GENERATED_OUTPUT = Path("ni07_utm_v3_synthetic_readout.json")`, a CWD-relative path, even though the test takes `tmp_path`. Line 124 opens it with `GENERATED_OUTPUT.open("x")`, which is exclusive-create, so a second run in the same checkout raises FileExistsError. The file is sitting untracked at the repo root right now (`git status`: `?? ni07_utm_v3_synthetic_readout.json`), so the next run of `test_eight_synthetic_classes_have_only_source_supported_store_expectations` in this checkout fails. test_graph_compaction_budgets.py sets ORCHESTRATOR_PATHS_TMP_DIR=tmp_path (418) but then patches `src.config.get_config` with a bare MagicMock (438, 520). compaction.py:112 calls `Path(get_config().paths.tmp_dir)`, and MagicMock.__fspath__ returns 'MagicMock/<name>/<id>', so files like `MagicMock/mock.paths.tmp_dir/<object-id>/session_compaction-error_ctx_0.md` now exist in the repo root. The env var is dead in these tests because it is only read in the except branch at :114. Instead of a fix, `.gitignore:137-138` added '# Test artifacts (MagicMock-as-path, etc.)\nMagicMock/'. `tmp/delegation_reports/coder_escalation-*.txt` also appears because delegation_reports.py:22 defaults to project_root/tmp.
proposal: (1) Write the UTM readout to `tmp_path / GENERATED_OUTPUT.name`. (2) In the compaction tests set `cfg.paths.tmp_dir = tmp_path` (or use create_autospec of the real config; see A6-15), then delete the MagicMock/ entry from .gitignore. (3) Add a `pytest_sessionstart`/`pytest_sessionfinish` pair to tests/conftest.py that snapshots `git status --porcelain --ignored --untracked-files=all` for the repo root and fails the session on any new path. This one guard catches every future CWD leak, and CI runs it automatically.
CORRECTED: (1) Make the readout path injectable: `Path(os.environ.get('NI07_UTM_READOUT', tmp_path / 'ni07_utm_v3_synthetic_readout.json'))`, keeping open('x'). Have ni07_utm_v3_capture.py set NI07_UTM_READOUT=<app>/ni07_utm_v3_synthetic_readout.json. That keeps the custody semantics, and local runs become idempotent. (2) Same as proposed: give the compaction tests a real tmp_dir, then drop MagicMock/ from .gitignore. (3) The session guard must use `git status --porcelain --ignored --untracked-files=all`, because every leaked path above is gitignored and a plain status shows none of them.
verify-note: The leaks are real. test_utm_v3_conformance.py:15 uses a CWD-relative path and :124 opens it with open('x'). ni07_utm_v3_synthetic_readout.json is untracked in this checkout. compaction.py:110-115 reads ORCHESTRATOR_PATHS_TMP_DIR only in the except branch, and MagicMock/{get_config().paths.tmp_dir,mock.paths.tmp_dir} exist in the repo root. tmp/delegation_reports/coder_escalation-*.txt exists. However, the CWD-relative, exclusive-create output is deliberate. epyc-root/.github/workflows/ni07-utm-v3.yml runs scripts/ci/ni07_utm_v3_capture.py, which runs pytest with cwd=app and `--generated-output ni07_utm_v3_synthetic_readout.json`. It raises if app/ni07_utm_v3_synthetic_readout.json already exists before execution, because exclusive create is its freshness proof, and it then uploads that file. The finder's fix (1) would break that capture recipe. The leak set is also larger than reported. The same run left logs/{serving_calls,progress,canvases}, data/trace/events.sqlite (228 events), orchestration/eval_instrument_ledger.json, orchestration/procedures/state/test_echo_*.json, orchestration/repl_memory/sessions/{episodic.db,session_pickle_hmac.key} and tmp/delegation_reports. All of them are gitignored.
verification: Run `pytest -p no:cacheprovider tests/unit/test_utm_v3_conformance.py tests/unit/test_graph_compaction_budgets.py` twice in a clean checkout. Both runs should pass, and `git status --porcelain --ignored` should show no new MagicMock/, ni07_*.json or tmp/ entries.

## A6-09 [sev 3] [tests/bug] [effort M] [behav intended] [partially] loc_delta=-20
**Tests use live production state as fixtures and fixed-name directories in production tmp (cross-session collisions, off-host ERRORs)**
files: O:tests/unit/test_e8_quality_baseline_v5.py:40, O:tests/unit/test_e8_quality_baseline_v5.py:94, O:tests/unit/test_e8_quality_baseline_v5.py:1977, O:tests/unit/test_repl_file_exploration.py:39, O:tests/unit/test_repl_file_mutation.py:44, O:tests/unit/test_output_spill.py:34, O:tests/unit/test_design_archive.py:19
evidence: test_e8_quality_baseline_v5.py:40 sets CANONICAL_STATE_PATH = '/mnt/raid0/llm/epyc-orchestrator/orchestration/autopilot_state.json'. That is an untracked file the running autopilot rewrites (`git ls-files` shows nothing), and it is read 7 times (94, 1975, 2019, 2045, 2079, 2390, 2431) with no skip guard. Off-host this raises FileNotFoundError, i.e. an ERROR, not a skip. The `_e7_pre_state` docstring (:79-92) records that the live file drifted (operator seed 2026-08-04, E9 on 2026-08-05) and the fixture had to be patched to compensate. ## A6-10 [sev 3] [modularity/refactor] [effort M] [behav none] [partially] loc_delta=-200
**Hermeticity is a patchwork of per-incident fixtures; replace it with one hermetic-by-default harness**
files: O:tests/conftest.py:117, O:tests/conftest.py:156, O:tests/conftest.py:178, O:tests/conftest.py:199, O:tests/conftest.py:220, O:tests/conftest.py:251, O:tests/unit/conftest.py:1, O:tests/unit/test_config_consolidation.py:16
evidence: The root conftest has 5 session-scoped env fixtures with identical save/set/restore bodies (lines 117 runtime flags, 156 KB-RAG telemetry, 178 serving-calls log, 199 GPU window, 220 context limits), about 20 lines each plus 10-30 line incident docstrings, plus `_hermetic_long_prefill_lease` (251). tests/unit/conftest.py adds 3 more (inference tap, KV-slot IO, inference lock + contention gate) after a 70-line docstring. Each fixture was added after a production leak (e.g. 738 fake tap sections, 11.2 GiB of kv_migrate files). The suite also holds 256 monkeypatches of module-level path constants (_AUTOPILOT_JOURNAL_PATH 30, STACK_PRIORS_PATH 27, STATE_PATH 20, LOG_DIR 14, ...). The unit conftest rejects an env-root seam because tests 'assert the REAL default paths' (test_config_consolidation::test_all_paths_on_raid). Those tests are already gated by `_on_raid` (test_config_consolidation.py:16) and already read ORCHESTRATOR_PATHS_LLM_ROOT (:702), so they can simply delenv. The in-progress working-tree change (fact a) adds two more import-time special cases to conftest.
proposal: Create tests/_hermetic.py, imported first by tests/conftest.py before any src import. It sets an env table once per session: ORCHESTRATOR_PATHS_LLM_ROOT and ORCHESTRATOR_PATHS_KERNEL_ROOT to a tmp skeleton (tmp/, cache/kv_slots/, logs/, models/, kernels/production/{cpu,gpu}/<stub binaries>), plus `{RUNTIME_FLAGS_ENV: neutral.json, kb LOG_ENV: 'off', serving LOG_ENV: 'off', gpu PATH_ENV: 'off', context LIVE_ENV: 'off', INFERENCE_TAP_FILE: unset}`. Keep the 3 function-scoped resets (config, context-limit resolver, prefix history) and the opt-out markers. Collapse the 5 session fixtures into one table-driven fixture. Production paths then become unreachable by default, so a new producer is hermetic without a new incident. The @_on_raid default-path tests call monkeypatch.delenv(...) and reset_config().
CORRECTED: Set the session env table in a module imported before any src import, so collection-time caches and runtime agree (this addresses the autokernel_enrollment desync). Keep the session skeleton outside tmp_path. Keep the function-scoped reset_config wrapper tolerant of a plain-callable get_config. Move the three unit-only fixtures to the root conftest so integration and top-level tests get them. Add AUTOPILOT_EVAL_INSTRUMENT_LEDGER and a trace-DB env seam to the table. Land it test-file-by-test-file with an unchanged on-host pass set, not as a single swap.
verify-note: Fixture line numbers match HEAD (session env fixtures at 117/156/178/199/220, _hermetic_long_prefill_lease at 251). The unit conftest has 3 more after a 70-line docstring, and the 256 path-constant monkeypatches are plausible. The proposal skips the evidence against it, however. tests/unit/conftest.py:26-46 documents that an earlier env-var seam (setenv ORCHESTRATOR_PATHS_TMP_DIR plus reset_config) broke four classes of tests: tmp_path-listing tests, test_all_paths_on_raid, test_autokernel_enrollment (collection-time cached values desynced) and tests that replace get_config with a plain callable. The finder addresses only one of them, so 'behavior_change: none' is optimistic. Also, the three unit-only hermetic fixtures (inference tap, KV-slot IO, inference lock) do not apply to the 72 non-unit files (52 top-level plus 20 integration). The env table also omits two live stores that tests do pollute (see missed A6-M1).
verification: Run `pytest tests -n 4` on host and off-host: same pass set. During a host run, `inotifywait -r /mnt/raid0/llm/tmp /mnt/raid0/llm/cache/kv_slots` (or an strace -f -e trace=openat filter) should show no pytest-PID writes. Conftest LOC drops by about 200.

## A6-M1 [sev 3] [tests/bug] [effort S] [behav intended] [verifier-added] loc_delta=25
**Tests write fake rows into live runtime stores that no hermetic fixture covers (trace events.sqlite, eval instrument ledger); a later test then fails depending on run order**
files: O:scripts/autopilot/experiment_journal.py:1123, O:src/trace/emit.py:145, O:src/trace/store.py:25, O:scripts/autopilot/eval_tower.py:130, O:tests/unit/test_era_stamp_reachability.py:318, O:tests/unit/test_eval_tower_instrument_repair.py:662, O:tests/test_trace_review_plane.py:317
evidence: A test run in this checkout (files dated 2026-10-07 04:25-05:00, after HEAD at 03:58) left two polluted stores. The first is data/trace/events.sqlite with 228 events: 219 with source='autopilot_live' and role 'test_species' ('trial 1 species=test_species tier=2 quality=1.4'), plus 9 with source='review_plane'. The writer is ExperimentJournal._emit_trace_event (experiment_journal.py:1123-1175), which calls emit() without db_path. emit's default `db_path=DEFAULT_DB_PATH` (emit.py:145) is bound at definition time to <repo>/data/trace/events.sqlite (store.py:25), and there is no env override or kill switch (grep for environ/PYTEST in emit.py, store.py and experiment_journal.py returns nothing). 27 of the 28 unit test files that construct ExperimentJournal never redirect it. The second is orchestration/eval_instrument_ledger.json, which holds entries for 'core-x', 'core-v', 'legacy_pool_seed_3_n7' and the production T1 id 'core_v2' (n_questions 1, tier_mix {'unknown': 1}). _INSTRUMENT_LEDGER_PATH is fixed at import (eval_tower.py:130-135). test_era_stamp_reachability.py:318-366 and test_eval_tower_instrument_repair.py:662-676 call _stamp_eval_instrument without monkeypatching it, while the neighboring test at :686 does. Concrete failures: (1) tests/test_trace_review_plane.py:317 test_tm1_materialized_db_smoke skips only when events.sqlite is absent, and otherwise asserts 'agent_audit' in stats()['by_source']. After any polluting test the file exists with only autopilot_live and review_plane, so the test fails on every later run in that checkout. (2) Run in the host's main checkout, the core_v2 overwrite makes the next real eval report instrument_drift_across_restart (content and tier-mix changed), a false measurement-instrument signal. Fake autopilot_live trials also become indistinguishable by source from real ones in the live trace store. All these paths are gitignored (.gitignore:30, :127), so `git status` without --ignored shows none of it.
proposal: In the hermetic harness, set AUTOPILOT_EVAL_INSTRUMENT_LEDGER (already honored by eval_tower.py:131) to a session tmp file before eval_tower is first imported. Add a call-time trace DB seam: make emit()/ensure_schema/stats take db_path=None and resolve `os.environ.get('ORCHESTRATOR_TRACE_DB') or DEFAULT_DB_PATH` when called, and set that variable in the harness. Make test_tm1_materialized_db_smoke opt-in on the host (a marker), not 'exists ⇒ assert'. The A6-02 session guard must include --ignored to catch the next store.
verify-note: 
verification: In a fresh checkout with no orchestration/eval_instrument_ledger.json or data/trace/: run `pytest -p no:cacheprovider tests/unit/test_era_stamp_reachability.py tests/unit/test_eval_tower_instrument_repair.py tests/unit/test_mutation_diagnostics.py tests/unit/test_autopilot_sequential_wiring.py tests/test_trace_review_plane.py`, twice. Both runs should pass (tm1 skipped), and `ls orchestration/eval_instrument_ledger.json data/trace/events.sqlite` should report both absent.

## A6-05 [sev 2] [modularity/refactor] [effort M] [behav none] [partially] loc_delta=-450
**357 sys.path inserts and 98 path-loaders load the same script under several module names (two distinct EvalResult classes proven in one process)**
files: O:tests/unit/test_core_v2_calibrate.py:13, O:tests/unit/test_diversity_metrics.py:28, O:scripts/autopilot/core_v2_calibrate.py:25, O:tests/unit/test_debugbench_diff_oracle.py:28, O:tests/unit/test_seed_specialist_routing_main_and_retry.py:54, O:tests/unit/test_safety_gate_speed_era_and_suite_absence.py:25, O:pyproject.toml:154
evidence: Metrics: 357 sys.path mutations (85x `REPO_ROOT/'scripts'/'autopilot'`, 57x `REPO_ROOT`, 15x scripts/benchmark, 13x scripts/server), 118 spec_from_file_location calls in 98 files, and 19 private `_load_module`-style helpers. With both paths inserted, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 .venv/bin/python -c 'import safety_gate as a; import scripts.autopilot.safety_gate as b; print(a is b, a.EvalResult is b.EvalResult, a.__file__==b.__file__)'` prints `False False True`: one file, two module objects, two dataclasses. test_core_v2_calibrate.py:13 builds `scripts.autopilot.safety_gate.EvalResult` and passes it to core_v2_calibrate, which annotates against the bare `safety_gate.EvalResult` (core_v2_calibrate.py:25). Dataclass __eq__ and isinstance are False across the two copies. debug_scorer.py is loaded bare in 11 files and also as 'dbg_scorer_under_test' (test_debugbench_diff_oracle.py:34) and 'lcb_scorer_under_test'. Production mixes names too: autopilot.py:9087 does `from host_health import` while safety_gate.py:2524 does `from scripts.autopilot.host_health import`, so a test monkeypatching one identity misses the other (test_gate_frontier_live_scope.py:27 comment admits the hazard).
proposal: Add `pythonpath = [".", "scripts/autopilot", "scripts/benchmark", "scripts/server"]` to [tool.pytest.ini_options] and delete every test-level `sys.path.insert` (the REPO_ROOT/ROOT/_ROOT boilerplate goes with them). Pick ONE canonical name per scripts module (bare, matching how autopilot.py runs). Rewrite the ~30 `scripts.autopilot.<mod>` test imports, and add a meta-test that fails if a module file appears in sys.modules under two names after collection. Replace the 19 `_load_module` helpers with one cached `tests/support/scripts.py::load_script(relpath)` that registers under the canonical name.
CORRECTED: Step 1, in production: normalize scripts/autopilot intra-package imports to one form (pick the bare form autopilot.py uses at runtime, or make scripts/autopilot a proper package) and remove the scripts' own sys.path.insert lines where the entrypoint already sets the path. Step 2: add pytest `pythonpath` and delete the test-side inserts. Step 3: only then add the duplicate-identity meta-test, scoped to scripts/autopilot modules. Present it as hygiene and monkeypatch-reliability work (a patch on one identity misses the other), not as a demonstrated isinstance bug.
verify-note: The counts hold: 367 sys.path insert/append lines in tests, and 118 spec_from_file_location calls in 98 files. Production mixes import identities (autopilot.py:9087 `from host_health import`, safety_gate.py:2524 `from scripts.autopilot.host_health import`). The headline example is harmless, though. core_v2_calibrate.py uses EvalResult only as a type annotation (lines 25 and 61), with no isinstance and no equality across copies, so test_core_v2_calibrate.py:13 cannot fail from the dual identity. Both host_health call sites only call HostHealthState.snapshot(). The proposed meta-test (no file loaded under two names) would fail on day one, because production modules themselves load both names and scripts self-insert sys.path at import (core_v2_calibrate.py:20-21). Deleting the test-side inserts does not change that. No scripts/autopilot module name collides with stdlib or site-packages, so the pythonpath ini change is safe.
verification: `grep -rn 'sys.path.insert' tests | wc -l` should be ~0. Add the meta-test (`len({m.__file__ for m in sys.modules.values() if getattr(m,'__file__',None)})` must equal the number of file-backed modules) and run it after a full collection. Run the full suite under xdist with the same pass count before and after.

A `ruff format` reflow (fact b) or extracting one call site breaks it with no behavior change. 9 sites slice `source[source.index("def _run_loop_inner("):]`, and test_autopilot_phase_status.py:1068/1084 asserts `'break' not in branch` and `'time.sleep(HEALTH_BACKOFF_S)' in branch` on `inspect.getsource(autopilot._run_loop_inner)`. `_run_loop_inner` (autopilot.py:8415) is 2,901 lines long, which is why tests resort to grep. The one behavioral driver, `_run_loop_inner_seq_harness` (test_autopilot_sequential_wiring.py:2035), is 452 lines with 41 monkeypatch.setattr calls. test_dashboard_panels.py:23,50 asserts `f'"{spec.key}"' in _DASHBOARD_SRC`. test_gpu_shadow_lane_p2.py:383-399 asserts import absence by substring.
proposal: Before the autopilot decomposition, convert each `_run_loop_inner` source assertion into a behavior test on the extracted seam it guards. Replace the import-absence substring tests with an import-linter `forbidden` contract (or ruff TID251 banned-module-level-imports), which takes one config stanza instead of tests. Replace the dashboard stamp-grep with a runtime check that each registered panel key appears in a stamped response.
CORRECTED: Keep the plan to convert the _run_loop_inner source asserts into seam-level behavior tests as acceptance tests for the decomposition. Drop the ruff-format rationale and the 'format branch' verification step. Instead, verify that each converted test fails when its guarded behavior is removed (mutation check) and passes after extraction.
verify-note: The decomposition hazard is real. _run_loop_inner spans autopilot.py:8415-11318 (about 2,900 lines). I counted 11 sites that slice or inspect.getsource it. test_autopilot_phase_status.py:1068-1090 greps branch text, and test_gate_frontier_c_and_b.py:215-228 splits on 'gate.update_baseline(\n'. The formatting claim is refuted. I ran `ruff format` (repo config) on a scratch copy of autopilot.py and re-ran both c_and_b assertions: still exactly 3 calls, and both checks pass. I also checked every quoted literal asserted `in source/body/branch/block` across the 10 test files that slice autopilot source: none disappears after formatting. Calling these tests 'format-sensitive today' is wrong. They only become fragile when code is extracted or moved.
verification: `grep -rnE '__file__\)\.read_text|inspect\.getsource\(|index\("def _run_loop_inner' tests | wc -l` should trend to 0. Apply `ruff format scripts/autopilot/autopilot.py` in a scratch branch and the converted tests should still pass (today test_gate_frontier_c_and_b is format-sensitive).

## A6-11 [sev 2] [best_practice/refactor] [effort S] [behav none] [confirmed] loc_delta=-120
**Unspecced MagicMock patches of features()/get_config() make every unset flag truthy and every path a MagicMock**
files: O:tests/unit/test_graph_compaction_budgets.py:434, O:tests/unit/test_chat_endpoints.py:654, O:src/features.py:1052, O:tests/unit/conftest.py:180
evidence: 55 `patch("src.features.features")` sites in 9 files and 15 bare `patch("src.config.get_config")` sites return MagicMock. Any attribute a test forgets to set is a truthy MagicMock, so a new flag consulted by the code under test is silently ON, and any path is MagicMock. That is the direct cause of the MagicMock/ directory in A6-02. The repo already has the correct seams: `set_features(Features(...))` (src/features.py:1052, used 126 times) and the `dataclasses.replace(cfg, paths=...)` wrapper pattern in tests/unit/conftest.py:180-183. `reset_features()` is wired by hand in 49 files instead of once in the root conftest.
proposal: Add two fixtures to tests/conftest.py. `features_override(**flags)` builds `dataclasses.replace(Features(), **flags)` and calls set_features (it raises TypeError on an unknown flag name, which a MagicMock never does). `config_override(**sections)` replaces sections on the real config. Make `reset_features()` part of the root autouse reset alongside reset_config and delete the 49 local copies. Ban `patch("src.features.features")`/`patch("src.config.get_config")` with a 10-line meta-test that greps tests/.
verify-note: Counted 55 `patch("src.features.features")` sites in 9 files and 15 bare `patch("src.config.get_config")` sites (test_context_compactor 5, model_server_coverage 4, model_server_extended 5, graph_compaction_budgets 1). Features is an @dataclass (src/features.py:471), so a dataclasses.replace-based override raises on an unknown flag. set_features and reset_features exist, and reset_features() is called by hand in 49 files. The MagicMock/ leak traces to the get_config MagicMock (compaction.py:112).
verification: `grep -rn 'patch("src.features.features")\|patch("src.config.get_config")' tests | wc -l` should be 0. Re-run the 9 affected files with an unchanged pass count. A deliberately misspelled flag in features_override should raise.

## A6-12 [sev 2] [tests/deletion] [effort S] [behav intended] [confirmed] loc_delta=-60
**Obsolete 'pending-CP' landing guards turn import regressions into silent skips**
files: O:tests/test_reviewer_contract.py:36, O:tests/test_reviewer_contract.py:46, O:tests/test_evidence_semantics.py:27, O:tests/test_evidence_loop.py:37, O:tests/test_decision_invalidation_contract.py:1, O:tests/test_candidate_security.py:1
evidence: test_reviewer_contract.py:36-41 has `_try_import` with `except Exception: return None`, then `requires_cp1_reducer = skipif(policy_reducer is None or not hasattr(policy_reducer,'reduce_decision'), reason='pending-CP1 ... not landed')`. These are the two 'swallowed broad except' hits in the metrics (test_reviewer_contract:39, test_evidence_semantics:27). Every guarded symbol has landed: policy_reducer.reduce_decision (src/proactive_delegation/policy_reducer.py:810), LoopState (:743), src/proactive_delegation/authority.py, orchestration/assurance_profile.schema.json, orchestration/evidence_item.schema.json, and review_ledger.invalidate_on_material_change (src/trace/review_ledger.py:1286). So the guards now only matter when an import FAILS: a SyntaxError or circular import in policy_reducer would show up as skips labeled 'pending-CP1'. ## A6-13 [sev 2] [best_practice/refactor] [effort S] [behav none] [confirmed] loc_delta=-470
**pytest configuration hygiene: no strict markers, no timeout, dead hooks, 447 redundant asyncio marks, split dev deps**
files: O:pyproject.toml:154, O:pyproject.toml:86, O:pyproject.toml:194, O:tests/integration/test_cache_hits.py:28, O:tests/integration/test_cache_integration.py:340, O:tests/conftest.py:72
evidence: [tool.pytest.ini_options] (pyproject:154-159) has no `--strict-markers`. `@pytest.mark.slow` is used but never registered, and markers are registered imperatively in two conftests instead of in pyproject. There is no `timeout` even though pytest-timeout is a dev dep, and the unit conftest itself documents tests blocking 180 s on the host inference lock. asyncio_mode='auto' is set, yet 447 `@pytest.mark.asyncio` decorators remain, all no-ops. `addopts = "-v"` prints one line per test for 16,777 tests. test_cache_hits.py:28-41 defines `pytest_configure`/`pytest_addoption` inside a test module; pytest ignores hooks there, so this is dead code duplicating the root option. `--run-server` is registered in the root conftest but enforced by hand-written autouse skip fixtures in two classes (test_cache_integration.py:340, test_cache_hits.py:202), unlike `--run-live-models`, which the collection hook handles. Dev deps are split: [project.optional-dependencies].dev has pytest/xdist/timeout and ruff>=0.8.0, while [dependency-groups].dev has only ruff>=0.15.10, so a default `uv sync` installs no pytest.
proposal: In pyproject set `addopts = "-q -ra --strict-markers"`, `timeout = 120`, and a `markers = [...]` list (heavy, integration, requires_server, requires_live_models, slow, host_paths, real_inference_lock_state, real_kv_slot_io, real_inference_tap). Handle `requires_server` in the conftest collection hook like requires_live_models and delete the per-class skip fixtures and the dead module hooks. Remove the 447 asyncio marks with a sed one-liner. Merge the test deps into [dependency-groups].dev (one ruff floor).
verify-note: pyproject [tool.pytest.ini_options] (:154-159) has no --strict-markers and no timeout, and addopts is '-v --tb=short'. There are 447 `@pytest.mark.asyncio` with asyncio_mode='auto'. pytest.mark.slow is used once and not registered in either conftest or pyproject. test_cache_hits.py:28-42 defines pytest_configure and pytest_addoption inside a test module (ignored by pytest, duplicating the root --run-server). pytest and pytest-timeout are only in [project.optional-dependencies].dev, while [dependency-groups].dev holds only ruff>=0.15.10, so a default `uv sync` installs no pytest.
verification: `pytest --collect-only -q tests` should collect with --strict-markers and no PytestUnknownMarkWarning. `grep -rc '@pytest.mark.asyncio' tests` should be 0 with an unchanged pass count. `uv sync && uv run pytest --version` should succeed.

Replace the patch pyramids with an ExitStack-based `chat_stage_stubs` fixture returning a dict of mocks, and assert stage call order (`assert_awaited_once`). Parametrize the 4 swap tests over a per-role expectation table. Split test_dashboard_helpers.py by the module it targets, with a `dashboard_paths(tmp_path)` fixture that redirects every producer path at once.
verify-note: test_build_report_blocks_on_underlying_fable5_gates spans test_model_gate_report.py:13-468 with 101 asserts, and :348 asserts a follow_up string beginning 'cd /mnt/raid0/llm/epyc-orchestrator && ...'. test_chat_endpoints.py:632 nests 9+ patch contexts and asserts only result.answer == 'REPL fallback', with no check that _execute_delegated was awaited, so the mutation scenario is plausible. The four swap tests exist at :943/:1062/:1177/:1312. test_dashboard_helpers.py is 4,346 LOC.
verification: The pass count should be unchanged, and the longest test function (metrics script) should be under 150 lines. Mutation check: delete the `_execute_delegated` call in src/api/routes/chat.py and the rewritten chat_endpoints test must fail (today it passes).

'Delete the file' discards genuine tests. test_no_orchestrator_imports (:115) is an AST walk of chat.py that fails on an 'orchestrator' import, not an assert on a constant. Genuine checks also include TestSignatureCompatibility (the 7b4b2da regression class), TestRoutingResult (4 tests, 12 asserts), TestRoleTimeouts (3), and AppState/HealthResponse wiring. TestAnnotateError (8 tests) can be deleted, but because it is tested twice more elsewhere (test_stages.py:110-140 and test_chat_pipeline_stages.py:213+), not because of import-smoke. A walk_packages import-everything test would trip on import-time side effects (fact (a): health_tracker, stack_manifest) and on optional deps (pyproject coverage omits src/gradio_ui.py as 'not testable in CI'), so it needs an explicit exclude list.
